(aux hakaru10): the language

Introduction

Quoting from Embedded probabilistic domain-specific language Hakaru10:

❝
This system description paper introduces the probabilistic programming language Hakaru10, for expressing, and performing inference on (general) graphical models. The language supports discrete and continuous distributions, mixture distributions and conditioning. Hakaru10 is a DSL embedded in Haskell and supports Monte-Carlo Markov Chain (MCMC) inference. Hakaru10 is designed to address two main challenges of probabilistic programming: performance and correctness. It implements the incremental Metropolis-Hastings method, avoiding all redundant computations. In the presence of conditional branches, efficiently maintaining dependencies and correctly computing the acceptance ratio are non-trivial problems, solved in Hakaru10. The implementation is unique in being explicitly designed to satisfy the common equational laws of probabilistic programs. Hakaru10 is typed; specifically, its type system statically prevents meaningless conditioning, enforcing that the values to condition upon must indeed come from outside the model.

Oleg Kiselyov

The paper is Probabilistic Programming Language and its Incremental Evaluation, APLAS 2016, LNCS 10017 (design.pdf, abstract at design.txt l.8-23). This is a port of the November 2016 code (index.html:52-53; the page was last updated on July 14, 2018) to CHICKEN Scheme 6: the modules (aux hakaru10 random) (the random-1.0.1.1 StdGen of GHC, so that upstream's recorded runs replay bit for bit), (aux hakaru10 distribution) (Distribution.hs), (aux hakaru10 util) (Util.hs), (aux hakaru10 engine) (Metropolis.hs and Memory.hs, and the internals of Syntax.hs), (aux hakaru10 exact) (new: exact enumeration, a forward sampler and a reference evaluator, the oracles of the tests) and the public (aux hakaru10) (Syntax.hs). Every definition carries a cf. comment naming the upstream lines it transcribes, every departure a numbered deviation comment (the register is in the section on findings below). Citations read M:404-419 for Metropolis.hs lines 404-419, and likewise S Syntax.hs, Mem Memory.hs, D Distribution.hs, U Util.hs, E Examples1.hs, CB ColoredBalls.hs, B1 Birds1.hs, BE Bench.hs, W index.html, BMP BirdMigrationProblem.txt and P the paper's text (design.txt), all from okmij.org/ftp/kakuritu/Hakaru10, and R the System/Random.hs of random-1.0.1.1 (hackage, 567 lines), whose StdGen GHC used for upstream's recorded runs.

In Scheme a model is a State computation (S:82, Model a = MCMCM (SExp a), MCMCM a = State MCState a, M:200) written with do/monad, as in Syntax.hs: its state is the chain, and running it builds the trace graph, dist, diracN and if_ each adding their nodes. The same grass model below is sampled by incremental Metropolis-Hastings (mcmC), enumerated exactly over its trace graph into a Hansei search tree (exact-posterior) and sampled forward over the same graph with likelihood weights (likelihood-weighting).

(define (nnot p b) (if b p 1.0))

(define ((nor strength-x strength-y noise) x y)
  (bern (- 1.0
           (* (* (nnot (- 1.0 strength-x) x) (nnot (- 1.0 strength-y) y))
              (- 1.0 noise)))))

(define grass
  (do/monad
    (← rain (dist bern 0.3))
    (← sprinkler (dist bern 0.5))
    (← grass-is-wet (dist (condition #t (nor 0.9 0.8 0.1)) rain sprinkler))
    (return rain)))

(((mcmC 10 grass) (#t #t #t #f #f #f #f #f #f #f))
 ((exact-posterior grass) ((#f . 0.53152855727963) (#t . 0.46847144272037)))
 ((likelihood-weighting 2000 grass)
  ((#f . 0.53654028664459) (#t . 0.46345971335541))))

Hakaru10 by example

The tutorial of P §2.1 (l.202-327), with each Haskell model next to its Scheme transcription in the cases of the replay and laws suites. Haskell's do is do/monad, x <- m is (← x m) and return e is (return e); a model is a value, defined with define as in Haskell, and a model with arguments a procedure answering one. Quoting P l.204-219:

❝
The first, elementary program is pbern = dist bern 0.4 whose inferred type is Model Bool. The program represents the model consisting of a single Boolean ('Bernoulli') random variable, whose distribution is True with the probability 40% and False with the probability 60%. [...] The function mcmC, the interpreter of probabilistic programs, [...] performs the MH inference on a model and returns the list of samples from the model's distribution. In particular, mcmC 10 pbern produces a list of 10 booleans, which indeed contains 4 True and 6 False values.

Oleg Kiselyov

(define (pbern* bern) (dist bern 0.4))

(define pnorm (dist normal 10.0 0.5))

(define pdep1 (do/monad (← x pnorm) (diracN (sexp+ x 1.0))))

The Dirac distribution is the unit of model composition (P l.255-261, §4.3 l.803-808), for every model p and expression e:

do {x ← p; diracN x} ≡ p,  do {x ← diracN e; p} ≡ let x = e in p

and independent variables marginalize (P l.273-281): pmarg has the distribution of pnorm. The hierarchical model of Hur et al. [10, Fig. 3] (P l.283-300), on which STAN infers a wrong distribution, is the 10th iterate of a model transformer; in Scheme, where models are values, it is a fold:

(define (deeper m) (do/monad (← x m) (dist normal x 3.0)))

(define (phierN n) (fold (λ (_ m) (deeper m)) (dist normal 0.0 1.0) (iota n)))

Branching models

Quoting P §2.2 l.330-347:

❝
Hakaru10 can express not only 'straight-line' but also branching models. An example is a simple mixture model [mixng] whose distribution is the mixture of normal and gamma distributions, with the random variable x determining the proportion of the mixture. [...] The mixng program is the first betraying Model being not quite a monad. The variable x is not actually of the type Double, as one might have thought. [...] As should be apparent from the use of Applicative operator <$>, which is just fmap, Model a is something like M (A a) where M is a monad but A is an applicative.

Oleg Kiselyov

In Scheme the applicative of SExp is lift (with pair, collect and the sexp+ family for the Num instance, S:99-135), while <$> and <*> are those of the Model monad (B1:52 abs <$> dist normal 0 10), and the branch is if_, whose test must be a boolean stochastic expression:

(define mixng
  (do/monad
    (← x (dist normal 0.0 1.0))
    (if_ (lift (λ (xv) (> xv 0.0)) x)
         (dist normal 10.0 2.0)
         (dist gamma 3.0 (/ 1.0 3.0)))))

(define (mixng′* bern)
  (do/monad
    (← x (dist bern 0.5))
    (if_ x (dist normal 10.0 2.0) (dist gamma 3.0 (/ 1.0 3.0)))))

Both should give the same distribution, as should the interleaving of 2500 samples of each component (P l.361-366; replay case X12).

Conditioning

Quoting P §2.3 l.393-398 and l.418-421:

❝
As biased coin code demonstrates, in Hakaru10 conditioning may only be applied to distributions. One may think that (c1 `condition` bern) creates a new distribution out of bern, with the singular value c1. Conditioning on arbitrary boolean formulas is fraught with peril, both theoretical and practical, degenerating MCMC algorithm into the inefficient rejection sampling. [...] Here, we attempted to condition bern on the random choice within the model. This is not allowed: observations must be external to the model. In [20], this semantic condition was a merely a coding convention, whose violation manifested as a run-time exception. In Hakaru10, the violation is a type error.

Oleg Kiselyov

(define (biased-coin c1 c2)
  (do/monad
    (← b (dist beta 1.0 1.0))
    (dist (condition c1 bern) b)
    (dist (condition c2 bern) b)
    (return b)))

Why distributions: the temperature model of P l.653-673 observes 25 from normal tempr 0.1, an event of probability zero; what can be asked is how likely 25 is under that distribution, its density, which the observed node contributes to the trace's log-likelihood. Scheme has no static types, so the static discipline of Syntax.hs becomes run-time checks (deviation 9, in the style of Ścibior et al. [20]), each an (exn hakaru10) condition whose kind property says what was violated (case test/language/errors):

  • observations are external (S:38-42, S:174-193): condition of a stochastic expression, of a model, or of a Scheme value holding either raises conditioning, and so does an observation that a distribution constructor computes from its argument, at the update that changes it;
  • only resampleable distributions are conditioned, never twice (S:185-193, E:180-186): conditioning;
  • SExp is not a monad (S:118, P l.739-747): a model answered by a lifted function, at the build or at an update, raises construction;
  • a model is a type of its own (S:82, plan §15 C32): a model in an SExp position (an argument of dist, the result, inside a Scheme value) or a value where a model is expected (mcmC's argument, a branch of if_) raises construction; procedures are plain values (S:107 Val f);
  • the test of if_ is SExp Bool (S:275): a non-boolean raises branch;
  • lexical scoping, implicit upstream (deviation 10): a node of another chain raises construction, a node escaping its branch, as a parent or as the result of another branch or of the model, branch;
  • the static kind of a node (M:46-60; deviation 10): a constructor that changes kind raises construction.

The conditioned grass model of P §1 (l.73-89) and its posterior P(rain | wet) = 0.4684714427203697 (E:322) close the tutorial; see the first figure above.

Implementation

Quoting P §4.1 l.723-747:

❝
Hakaru10 represents the trace -- random variables of the model and their dependencies -- as a directed acyclic graph (DAG). Each node (vertex) in the graph stands for one random variable (whose value can be sampled and resampled) or an observed variable, whose value cannot be resampled. There are also computational nodes, representing 'samples' from the dirac distribution. [...] A Hakaru10 model has the type type Model a = MCMCM (SExp a) It is a computation that constructs the trace graph. MCMCM is a monad, but SExp is not. It is an applicative [13]: it lets us construct new graph nodes, without looking at their values. [...] The fact that SExp is not a monad is significant: the current node values cannot influence the graph construction. Therefore, after the graph is built, its structure does not change. All dependencies among nodes can be computed once and for all.

Oleg Kiselyov

In (aux hakaru10 engine) the trace is a chain, a vector of node records indexed by creation order (the address, replacing Memory.hs); a node's dynamic state is an immutable nstate record (value, log-likelihood, distribution, timestamp) replaced as a whole, as Mem.modify_ref does. Model a = MCMCM (SExp a) is a State monad threading the chain (plan §15 C34), run once per build by build-chain (M:872-880 initial_step'), whose construction functions mirror Syntax.hs and Metropolis.hs by name (create-node, with-node-ctor, to-node, ifnode, ...); an SExp is a node, an nt (leaves and a function: S:85-116's NT) or any other value (Val). For the grass model the graph has the three nodes of P's Fig. 1 (case test/language/graph/grass).

Incremental recomputation (P §4.2 l.765-800): resampling a node enqueues its dependents; propagate! (M:293-317) pops the queue in increasing address order, a binary heap, because

❝
The fact that Hakaru10 models are acyclic and Hakaru10 programs are declarative (with no mutations) lets us avoid the topological sort and rely on the 'creation times' of trace nodes. A node can only depend on those constructed before it.

Oleg Kiselyov

Creation order is therefore load-bearing (plan C18): a node is created when the model that makes it runs, in bind order, do/monad top to bottom and the traversals head first (plan §15 C35; see the embedding notes).

The Dirac distribution (P §4.3 l.801-843): a Dirac node is a pure computation, recomputed whenever a parent changes, its log-likelihood staying 0, so a proposal is extended to all dependent Dirac nodes and the unit laws hold by construction (laws suite). diracN of a node answers the node itself, shared (S:164-168).

Branching (P §4.4 l.844-869):

❝
Hakaru10 avoids any modifications to the graph structure during the trace update. It compiles both if-branches when constructing the initial graph. [...] Updating the nodes in the inactive branch, whose values will be ultimately ignored, is not good for performance. Therefore, we mark the nodes in the non-current branch as inactive, and delay their updates until they are activated. Thus each node, along with its current value, LL and the distribution also keeps the inactivation count.

Oleg Kiselyov

if_ (S:275-279) builds the entry (the test, always a new Dirac node); ifnode (M:641-686) then builds the inactive branch first, deactivates its nodes (deactivate, M:696-717, which takes the observed nodes' LL back), the active branch, and the exit node, whose value is the active branch's result (update-exit, M:778-794). When the entry's value changes, update-entry (M:760-775) moves the counts and the observed LL between the branches and replays the updates delayed while a node was inactive.

One MH step, transit! (M:908-945): pick an active resampleable node uniformly (M:956-977), resample it from its own distribution, propagate, and accept with the log ratio of M:929-934, where Δ is the change of the trace's log-likelihood accumulated by the updates, S the number of resampleable nodes and c and c′ the change of their active count before and after the step (the derivation is the note M:1039-1089):

log α = Δ − ( fwd − bwd ) + log S + c S + c′

A rejected step restores the whole state from a journal of undo thunks, except the random generator (M:904-906). The engine's source:

(module
  (aux hakaru10 engine)
  *
  (import
    scheme
    (only (scheme base)
          vector-map
          vector-for-each
          make-parameter
          exact-integer?)
    (chicken base)
    (chicken fixnum)
    (chicken flonum)
    (only (chicken format) fprintf)
    (only (chicken port) with-output-to-port)
    (only (chicken sort) sort!)
    (only (chicken condition)
          make-property-condition
          signal
          condition-case
          with-exception-handler
          condition-predicate
          condition-property-accessor)
    (only srfi-1 any every find delete-duplicates append-map list-index iota)
    (only srfi-69 make-hash-table hash-table-exists? hash-table-set!)
    (aux base)
    (only (aux category state)
          category-state?
          state
          run-state
          eval-state
          exec-state
          get
          gets
          modify
          return
          >>=)
    (only (aux category monad state) do/monad ▷ when/monad)
    (aux hakaru10 random)
    (aux hakaru10 distribution))
  (declare (local))
  (define-record nstate val ll dst tstamp)
  (set-record-printer!
    nstate
    (λ (s port)
       (fprintf
         port
         "#<nstate ~s ll ~s t ~s>"
         (nstate-val s)
         (nstate-ll s)
         (nstate-tstamp s))))
  (define-record
    node
    chain
    addr
    kind
    role
    parents
    fn
    update
    scope
    deps
    branch
    state
    inactive
    delayed
    pending
    queued)
  (set-record-printer!
    node
    (λ (n port) (fprintf port "#<node ~a ~a>" (node-addr n) (node-kind n))))
  (define (node-value n) (nstate-val (node-state n)))
  (define (node-inactive? n) (fx> (node-inactive n) 0))
  (define-record
    chain
    rng
    semantics
    now
    nodes
    count
    llcum
    sampleable
    samp
    card-change
    queue
    pending
    journal
    scope
    step-hook)
  (set-record-printer!
    chain
    (λ (ch port)
       (fprintf
         port
         "#<chain ~a nodes, now ~a>"
         (chain-count ch)
         (chain-now ch))))
  (define (new-chain src semantics)
    (unless
      (memq semantics '(upstream extended))
      (hakaru10-error
        'construction
        'mcmC
        "semantics must be 'upstream or 'extended"
        semantics))
    (make-chain
      src
      semantics
      1
      (make-vector 64 #f)
      0
      0.0
      '()
      (vector)
      0
      #f
      '()
      #f
      '()
      #f))
  (define (chain-node ch a) (vector-ref (chain-nodes ch) a))
  (define (chain-store! ch n)
    (let ((a (node-addr n)) (v (chain-nodes ch)))
      (when (fx>= a (vector-length v))
            (chain-nodes-set!
              ch
              (vector-resize v (fx* 2 (vector-length v)) #f)))
      (vector-set! (chain-nodes ch) a n)
      (chain-count-set! ch a)))
  (define (chain-fold kons knil ch #!optional (lo 0) (hi (chain-count ch)))
    (let loop ((a (fx+ lo 1)) (acc knil))
      (if (fx> a hi) acc (loop (fx+ a 1) (kons (chain-node ch a) acc)))))
  (define (chain-freeze! ch)
    (chain-samp-set! ch (list->vector (chain-sampleable ch)))
    (chain-queue-set!
      ch
      (make-update-queue (make-vector (fx+ 1 (chain-count ch)) #f) 0))
    (chain-pending-set! ch '())
    (chain-scope-set! ch '()))
  (define (extended? ch) (eq? (chain-semantics ch) 'extended))
  (define (journal-begin! ch)
    (when (chain-journal ch) (journal-rollback! ch))
    (let ((now (chain-now ch)) (card (chain-card-change ch)))
      (chain-journal-set!
        ch
        (list (τ (chain-now-set! ch now) (chain-card-change-set! ch card))))))
  (define (journal-commit! ch) (chain-journal-set! ch #f))
  (define (journal-rollback! ch)
    (for-each (λ (undo) (undo)) (chain-journal ch))
    (chain-journal-set! ch #f)
    (chain-pending-set! ch '())
    (update-queue-clear! (chain-queue ch)))
  (define-syntax-rule
    (journaled-set! (getter setter) n v)
    (let* ((n* n)
           (ch (node-chain n*))
           (old (getter n*))
           (journal (chain-journal ch)))
      (when journal (chain-journal-set! ch (cons (τ (setter n* old)) journal)))
      (setter n* v)))
  (define (set-node-state! n s)
    (journaled-set! (node-state node-state-set!) n s))
  (define (set-inactive! n k)
    (journaled-set! (node-inactive node-inactive-set!) n k))
  (define (set-delayed! n flag)
    (unless
      (eq? flag (node-delayed n))
      (journaled-set! (node-delayed node-delayed-set!) n flag)))
  (define (set-pending! n flag)
    (unless
      (eq? flag (node-pending n))
      (journaled-set! (node-pending node-pending-set!) n flag)
      (when flag
            (let1 (ch (node-chain n))
                  (chain-pending-set! ch (cons n (chain-pending ch)))))))
  (define-record update-queue heap size)
  (define-syntax-rule (node<? a b) (fx< (node-addr a) (node-addr b)))
  (define (enqueue! q n)
    (unless
      (node-queued n)
      (node-queued-set! n #t)
      (let ((heap (update-queue-heap q)) (i (update-queue-size q)))
        (update-queue-size-set! q (fx+ i 1))
        (let up ((i i))
          (if (fx= i 0)
            (vector-set! heap 0 n)
            (let* ((p (fxshr (fx- i 1) 1)) (parent (vector-ref heap p)))
              (if (node<? n parent)
                (begin (vector-set! heap i parent) (up p))
                (vector-set! heap i n))))))))
  (define (dequeue! q)
    (let* ((heap (update-queue-heap q))
           (size (fx- (update-queue-size q) 1))
           (top (vector-ref heap 0))
           (last (vector-ref heap size)))
      (update-queue-size-set! q size)
      (node-queued-set! top #f)
      (let down ((i 0))
        (let* ((l (fx+ 1 (fx* 2 i))) (r (fx+ l 1)))
          (if (fx>= l size)
            (vector-set! heap i last)
            (let* ((c (if (and (fx< r size)
                               (node<? (vector-ref heap r) (vector-ref heap l)))
                        r
                        l))
                   (child (vector-ref heap c)))
              (if (node<? child last)
                (begin (vector-set! heap i child) (down c))
                (vector-set! heap i last))))))
      top))
  (define (update-queue-empty? q) (fx= 0 (update-queue-size q)))
  (define (update-queue-clear! q)
    (let ((heap (update-queue-heap q)))
      (do ((i 0 (fx+ i 1)))
          ((fx= i (update-queue-size q)))
        (node-queued-set! (vector-ref heap i) #f))
      (update-queue-size-set! q 0)))
  (define (enqueue-all! ch nodes)
    (let1 (q (chain-queue ch)) (for-each (λ (n) (enqueue! q n)) nodes)))
  (define-record nt leaves fn)
  (set-record-printer!
    nt
    (λ (e port)
       (fprintf port "#<nt ~s>" (map node-addr (vector->list (nt-leaves e))))))
  (define (sexp? x) (or (node? x) (nt? x)))
  (define (sexp-chain e)
    (node-chain (if (node? e) e (vector-ref (nt-leaves e) 0))))
  (define (sexp-leaves e)
    (cond ((node? e) (vector e)) ((nt? e) (nt-leaves e)) (else (vector))))
  (define (leaf-values leaves) (vector-map node-value leaves))
  (define-record deferred fn args)
  (set-record-printer! deferred (λ (d port) (fprintf port "#<deferred>")))
  (define (deferrable? e) (or (not (hakaru10-error? e)) (parameter-error? e)))
  (define (force-value x)
    (if (deferred? x) ((deferred-fn x) (deferred-args x)) x))
  (define (first-deferred vs)
    (let loop ((i 0))
      (cond ((fx= i (vector-length vs)) #f)
            ((deferred? (vector-ref vs i)) (vector-ref vs i))
            (else (loop (fx+ i 1))))))
  (define (forced-values vs)
    (if (first-deferred vs) (vector-map force-value vs) vs))
  (define (forced-list vs) (if (any deferred? vs) (map force-value vs) vs))
  (define (call/deferring g args)
    (condition-case
      (g args)
      (e (exn) (if (deferrable? e) (make-deferred g args) (signal e)))))
  (define (lazy-value g vs inactive?)
    (if inactive?
      (or (first-deferred vs) (call/deferring g vs))
      (g (forced-values vs))))
  (define lazy-lifting (make-parameter #f))
  (define (sexp-value e)
    (cond ((node? e) (force-value (node-value e)))
          ((nt? e) ((nt-fn e) (forced-values (leaf-values (nt-leaves e)))))
          (else e)))
  (define (check-not-model who x)
    (when (model? x)
          (hakaru10-error
            'construction
            who
            "a model where an SExp is expected; bind it with ← first"
            x)))
  (define (internal-part x)
    (let1 (r (internal-part/bounded x 100000))
          (cond ((not (fixnum? r)) r)
                ((fx>= r 0) #f)
                (else (internal-part/cyclic x)))))
  (define (internal-part/bounded x budget)
    (cond ((fx< budget 0) budget)
          ((or (number? x) (null? x) (boolean? x) (symbol? x)) budget)
          ((pair? x)
           (let1 (r (internal-part/bounded (car x) (fx- budget 1)))
                 (if (fixnum? r) (internal-part/bounded (cdr x) r) r)))
          ((vector? x)
           (let loop ((i 0) (b (fx- budget 1)))
             (if (fx= i (vector-length x))
               b
               (let1 (r (internal-part/bounded (vector-ref x i) b))
                     (if (fixnum? r) (loop (fx+ i 1) r) r)))))
          ((or (sexp? x) (model? x)) x)
          (else budget)))
  (define (internal-part/cyclic x)
    (let1 (seen (make-hash-table eq?))
          (let walk ((x x))
            (cond ((or (pair? x) (vector? x))
                   (and (not (hash-table-exists? seen x))
                        (begin
                          (hash-table-set! seen x #t)
                          (if (pair? x)
                            (or (walk (car x)) (walk (cdr x)))
                            (any walk (vector->list x))))))
                  ((or (sexp? x) (model? x)) x)
                  (else #f)))))
  (define (check-no-sexp who x)
    (let1 (y (internal-part x))
          (cond ((sexp? y)
                 (hakaru10-error
                   'construction
                   who
                   "an SExp inside a Scheme value; combine with collect, pair or lift"
                   y))
                ((model? y)
                 (hakaru10-error
                   'construction
                   who
                   "a model inside a Scheme value"
                   y)))))
  (define (check-lifted-value who v #!optional deep?)
    (cond ((or (number? v) (boolean? v)) v)
          ((sexp? v)
           (hakaru10-error
             'construction
             who
             "a lifted function answered an SExp"
             v))
          ((model? v)
           (hakaru10-error
             'construction
             who
             "a lifted function answered a model"
             v))
          ((and deep? (or (pair? v) (vector? v))) (check-no-sexp who v) v)
          (else v)))
  (define (lift* f es #!optional (checked? #t))
    (for-each (λ (e) (check-not-model 'lift e)) es)
    (if (any sexp? es)
      (lift/fuse f es checked?)
      (let1 (v (lift/apply f es))
            (if checked? (check-lifted-value 'lift v #t) v))))
  (define (lift/apply f vs)
    (cond ((not (lazy-lifting)) (apply f (forced-list vs)))
          ((find deferred? vs))
          (else (call/deferring (λ (args) (apply f args)) vs))))
  (define (lift/fuse f es checked?)
    (let* ((leaves
             (delete-duplicates
               (append-map (λ (e) (vector->list (sexp-leaves e))) es)
               eq?))
           (owner (node-chain (car leaves)))
           (position (λ (n) (list-index (λ (m) (eq? n m)) leaves)))
           (extractors (map (λ (e) (lift/extractor position e)) es)))
      (unless
        (every (λ (n) (eq? (node-chain n) owner)) leaves)
        (hakaru10-error
          'construction
          'lift
          "nodes of different model runs"
          es))
      (make-nt
        (list->vector leaves)
        (if checked?
          (let1 (first? #t)
                (λ (vs)
                   (let1 (v (check-lifted-value
                              'lift
                              (apply f (map (λ (x) (x vs)) extractors))
                              first?))
                         (set! first? #f)
                         v)))
          (λ (vs) (apply f (map (λ (x) (x vs)) extractors)))))))
  (define (lift/extractor position e)
    (cond ((node? e) (let1 (i (position e)) (λ (vs) (vector-ref vs i))))
          ((nt? e)
           (let* ((g (nt-fn e))
                  (ps (map position (vector->list (nt-leaves e)))))
             (if (equal? ps (iota (length ps)))
               g
               (let1 (ps (list->vector ps))
                     (λ (vs) (g (vector-map (λ (p) (vector-ref vs p)) ps)))))))
          ((deferred? e) (λ (vs) (force-value e)))
          (else (K e))))
  (define model? category-state?)
  (define (check-model m who)
    (unless
      (model? m)
      (hakaru10-error
        'construction
        who
        "a model (a State computation: do/monad, dist, diracN, if_ or return) is expected"
        m))
    m)
  (define-syntax-rule (modify! (ch) body ...) (modify (λ (ch) body ... ch)))
  (define (hakaru10-fail kind who msg . irritants)
    (state (λ (_) (apply hakaru10-error kind who msg irritants))))
  (define (lazy-model who thunk)
    (state (λ (ch) (run-state (check-model (thunk) who) ch))))
  (define (condition/external obs d) (condition obs d internal-part))
  (define (check-owner ch e who)
    (when (and (sexp? e) (not (eq? (sexp-chain e) ch)))
          (hakaru10-error
            'construction
            who
            "a node of another model run (a stale node) is used"
            e)))
  (define (check-distk who dk)
    (unless
      (distk? dk)
      (hakaru10-error
        'construction
        who
        "a distribution constructor must return a distribution"
        dk))
    dk)
  (define (check-observation n obs)
    (unless
      (equal? obs (node-value n))
      (hakaru10-error
        'conditioning
        'update
        "observation must be external to the model"
        n
        (node-value n)
        obs)))
  (define (inactive-scope? ch)
    (any (λ (p) (not (eq? (node-value (car p)) (cdr p)))) (chain-scope ch)))
  (define (parameter-error? e) (eq? (hakaru10-error-kind e) 'parameter))
  (define (build-guard ch thunk)
    (if (and (pair? (chain-scope ch)) (extended? ch) (inactive-scope? ch))
      (condition-case
        (thunk)
        (e (exn)
           (signal
             (if (deferrable? e)
               (make-property-condition 'hakaru10-rejected-build)
               e))))
      (thunk)))
  (define (scope-tail? s s*)
    (let tail? ((cs s*)) (or (eq? cs s) (and (pair? cs) (tail? (cdr cs))))))
  (define (check-branch-escape parent child)
    (unless
      (or (eq? (node-role child) 'exit)
          (scope-tail? (node-scope parent) (node-scope child)))
      (hakaru10-error 'branch 'if_ "node escapes its branch" parent child)))
  (define (add-dependency! parent child)
    (check-branch-escape parent child)
    (unless
      (memq child (node-deps parent))
      (node-deps-set! parent (cons child (node-deps parent)))))
  (define (check-active-update n leaves)
    (vector-for-each
      (λ (p)
         (when (node-inactive? p)
               (hakaru10-error
                 'dependency
                 'update
                 "the parent or the node itself are inactive"
                 p
                 n)))
      leaves)
    (when (node-inactive? n)
          (hakaru10-error
            'dependency
            'update
            "the parent or the node itself are inactive"
            n)))
  (define (leaves-tstamp leaves)
    (let loop ((i 0) (ts 0))
      (if (fx= i (vector-length leaves))
        ts
        (loop (fx+ i 1)
              (fxmax ts (nstate-tstamp (node-state (vector-ref leaves i))))))))
  (define (doupdate n)
    (let ((ch (node-chain n)) (self (node-state n)) (leaves (node-parents n)))
      (unless (extended? ch) (check-active-update n leaves))
      (unless
        (fx> (leaves-tstamp leaves) (nstate-tstamp self))
        (hakaru10-error
          'dependency
          'update
          "dependency violation!"
          n
          (leaves-tstamp leaves)))
      (let* ((dk (check-distk 'update ((node-fn n) (leaf-values leaves))))
             (kind (distk-kind dk)))
        (unless
          (eq? kind (node-kind n))
          (hakaru10-error
            'construction
            'update
            "a distribution constructor changed the kind of its node"
            n
            (node-kind n)
            kind))
        (values
          (if (eq? kind 'dirac)
            (make-nstate
              (check-lifted-value 'update (distk-value dk))
              0.0
              #f
              (chain-now ch))
            (let ((rd (distk-rd dk)) (v (nstate-val self)))
              (when (eq? kind 'observed)
                    (check-observation n (distk-value dk)))
              (make-nstate
                v
                ((rdistr-log-density rd) v)
                rd
                (nstate-tstamp self))))
          '()))))
  (define (new-nref n)
    (state (λ (ch)
              (chain-store! ch n)
              (when (eq? (node-kind n) 'resampleable)
                    (chain-sampleable-set! ch (cons n (chain-sampleable ch))))
              (chain-llcum-set!
                ch
                (fp+ (chain-llcum ch) (nstate-ll (node-state n))))
              (cons n ch))))
  (define (new-dependency parent child)
    (modify! (ch) (add-dependency! parent child)))
  (define (register-dependencies leaves child)
    (modify! (ch) (vector-for-each (λ (p) (add-dependency! p child)) leaves)))
  (define (create-node
           update
           dk
           #!optional
           (role 'root)
           (parents (vector))
           (fn dk))
    (state (λ (ch)
              (receive
                (v ll)
                (build-guard ch (τ (distk-sample dk (chain-rng ch))))
                (run-state
                  (new-nref
                    (make-node
                      ch
                      (fx+ 1 (chain-count ch))
                      (distk-kind dk)
                      role
                      parents
                      fn
                      update
                      (chain-scope ch)
                      '()
                      #f
                      (make-nstate v ll (distk-rd dk) (chain-now ch))
                      0
                      #f
                      #f
                      #f))
                  ch)))))
  (define (with-node-none dk) (create-node #f dk))
  (define (with-node-tree leaves fn #!optional (who 'dist))
    (state (λ (ch)
              (let1 (dk (check-distk
                          who
                          (build-guard ch (τ (fn (leaf-values leaves))))))
                    (when (eq? (distk-kind dk) 'dirac)
                          (check-no-sexp who (distk-value dk)))
                    (let* ((r (run-state
                                (create-node doupdate dk 'plain leaves fn)
                                ch))
                           (n (car r)))
                      (cons n
                            (exec-state
                              (register-dependencies leaves n)
                              (cdr r))))))))
  (define (with-node ndr f #!optional (who 'dist))
    (with-node-tree
      (vector ndr)
      (λ (vs) (f (force-value (vector-ref vs 0))))
      who))
  (define (lazy-tree leaves g df who)
    (do/monad
      (← ch get)
      (let self #f)
      (← n
         (with-node-tree
           leaves
           (λ (vs)
              (df (lazy-value
                    g
                    vs
                    (if self (node-inactive? self) (inactive-scope? ch)))))
           who))
      (let _ (set! self n))
      (return n)))
  (define (with-node-ctor e df #!optional (who 'dist) lazy)
    (check-not-model who e)
    (do/monad
      (gets (λ (ch) (check-owner ch e who)))
      (cond ((node? e)
             (if lazy
               (lazy-tree (vector e) (λ (vs) (vector-ref vs 0)) df who)
               (with-node e df who)))
            ((nt? e)
             (if lazy
               (lazy-tree (nt-leaves e) (nt-fn e) df who)
               (let1 (g (nt-fn e))
                     (with-node-tree
                       (nt-leaves e)
                       (λ (vs) (df (g (forced-values vs))))
                       who))))
            (else
             (check-no-sexp who e)
             (>>= (gets (λ (ch)
                           (check-distk
                             who
                             (build-guard ch (τ (df (force-value e)))))))
                  with-node-none)))))
  (define (to-node e #!optional (who 'to_node))
    (check-not-model who e)
    (cond ((node? e)
           (do/monad
             (gets (λ (ch)
                      (check-owner ch e who)
                      (unless
                        (scope-tail? (node-scope e) (chain-scope ch))
                        (hakaru10-error
                          'branch
                          who
                          "node escapes its branch"
                          e))))
             (return e)))
          ((nt? e) (with-node-ctor e dirac who #t))
          (else (check-no-sexp who e) (with-node-none (dirac e)))))
  (define (pair a b) (lift* cons (list a b) #f))
  (define (collect es) (lift* list es #f))
  (define-syntax-rule
    (ctor-apply applied? d args call)
    (check-distk
      'dist
      (if applied?
        call
        (let1 (dk (call/arity-check d (λ () call) args))
              (set! applied? #t)
              dk))))
  (define (call/arity-check d thunk args)
    (with-exception-handler
      (λ (e)
         (if (and (arity-condition? e) (memq d (exn-arguments e)))
           (hakaru10-error
             'construction
             'dist
             "wrong number of arguments for the distribution constructor"
             d
             args)
           (signal e)))
      thunk))
  (define arity-condition? (condition-predicate 'arity))
  (define exn-arguments (condition-property-accessor 'exn 'arguments '()))
  (define (dist d . args)
    (cond ((distk? d)
           (unless
             (null? args)
             (hakaru10-error
               'construction
               'dist
               "a distribution takes no arguments"
               d
               args)))
          ((procedure? d)
           (for-each
             (λ (a)
                (check-not-model 'dist a)
                (unless (sexp? a) (check-no-sexp 'dist a)))
             args))
          (else
           (hakaru10-error
             'construction
             'dist
             "a distribution or a distribution constructor is expected"
             d)))
    (let1 (applied? #f)
          (match/first
            args
            (()
             (if (distk? d)
               (with-node-none d)
               (with-node-ctor '() (λ (_) (ctor-apply applied? d '() (d))))))
            ((,x)
             (with-node-ctor x (λ (v) (ctor-apply applied? d (list v) (d v)))))
            ((,x ,y)
             (with-node-ctor
               (pair x y)
               (Λ ((,a unquote b))
                  (ctor-apply applied? d (list a b) (d a b)))))
            (else (with-node-ctor
                    (collect args)
                    (λ (vs) (ctor-apply applied? d vs (apply d vs))))))))
  (define (diracN e) (to-node e 'diracN))
  (define (if_* test th el)
    (check-not-model 'if_ test)
    (do/monad
      (← entry (with-node-ctor test (λ (v) (dirac (check-test 'if_ v))) 'if_))
      (modify! (ch) (node-role-set! entry 'entry))
      (ifnode
        entry
        (▷ (check-model th 'if_) to-node)
        (▷ (check-model el 'if_) to-node))))
  (define (model->trace m who) (▷ (check-model m who) to-node))
  (define-record branch entry then-result else-result then-range else-range)
  (set-record-printer!
    branch
    (λ (b port)
       (fprintf
         port
         "#<branch ~a ~a ~a>"
         (node-addr (branch-entry b))
         (node-addr (branch-then-result b))
         (node-addr (branch-else-result b)))))
  (define (branch-active-result b)
    (if (node-value (branch-entry b))
      (branch-then-result b)
      (branch-else-result b)))
  (define (check-test who v . irritants)
    (unless
      (boolean? v)
      (apply hakaru10-error
             'branch
             who
             "the test of if_ must be a boolean"
             v
             irritants))
    v)
  (define (move-activity!
           ch
           lo
           hi
           step
           switching
           observed-weight
           dirac-switched)
    (let loop ((a (fx+ lo 1)) (llc 0.0) (k 0))
      (if (fx> a hi)
        (values llc k)
        (let* ((n (chain-node ch a))
               (count (node-inactive n))
               (kind (node-kind n)))
          (when (fx< (fx+ count step) 0)
                (hakaru10-error
                  'internal
                  'activate
                  "activating an active node"
                  n))
          (set-inactive! n (fx+ count step))
          (if (fx= count switching)
            (loop (fx+ a 1)
                  (fp+ llc
                       (case kind
                         ((observed) (observed-weight n))
                         ((dirac) (dirac-switched n) 0.0)
                         (else 0.0)))
                  (if (eq? kind 'resampleable) (fx+ k 1) k))
            (loop (fx+ a 1) llc k))))))
  (define (deactivate! ch lo hi)
    (receive
      (llc k)
      (move-activity!
        ch
        lo
        hi
        1
        0
        (λ (n)
           (if (node-pending n)
             (begin (set-pending! n #f) 0.0)
             (nstate-ll (node-state n))))
        void)
      (chain-llcum-set! ch (fp- (chain-llcum ch) llc))
      (chain-card-change-set! ch (fx- (chain-card-change ch) k))))
  (define (activate! ch lo hi)
    (receive
      (llc k)
      (move-activity!
        ch
        lo
        hi
        -1
        1
        (if (extended? ch)
          (λ (n) (set-pending! n #t) 0.0)
          (λ (n) (nstate-ll (node-state n))))
        (λ (n)
           (when (deferred? (node-value n))
                 (chain-pending-set! ch (cons n (chain-pending ch))))))
      (chain-llcum-set! ch (fp+ (chain-llcum ch) llc))
      (chain-card-change-set! ch (fx+ (chain-card-change ch) k))))
  (define (settle-pending! ch)
    (let1 (pending (chain-pending ch))
          (when (pair? pending)
                (for-each
                  (λ (n)
                     (cond ((node-pending n)
                            (set-pending! n #f)
                            (chain-llcum-set!
                              ch
                              (fp+ (chain-llcum ch) (nstate-ll (node-state n)))))
                           ((and (eq? (node-kind n) 'dirac)
                                 (not (node-inactive? n)))
                            (let1 (s (node-state n))
                                  (when (deferred? (nstate-val s))
                                        (set-node-state!
                                          n
                                          (make-nstate
                                            (force-value (nstate-val s))
                                            (nstate-ll s)
                                            (nstate-dst s)
                                            (nstate-tstamp s))))))))
                  (sort! pending (λ (a b) (node<? a b))))
                (chain-pending-set! ch '()))))
  (define (switch-branches! ch s* extra newactive newinactive)
    (deactivate! ch (car newinactive) (cdr newinactive))
    (activate! ch (car newactive) (cdr newactive))
    (values
      s*
      (if (extended? ch)
        extra
        (chain-fold
          (λ (n deps)
             (if (node-delayed n)
               (begin (set-delayed! n #f) (cons n deps))
               deps))
          extra
          ch
          (car newactive)
          (cdr newactive)))))
  (define ((update-entry prev b) n)
    (let1 (old (node-value n))
          (receive
            (s* extra)
            (prev n)
            (let ((tv (nstate-val s*))
                  (ths (branch-then-range b))
                  (els (branch-else-range b)))
              (check-test 'if_ tv n)
              (cond ((eq? tv old) (values s* extra))
                    (tv (switch-branches! (node-chain n) s* extra ths els))
                    (else
                     (switch-branches! (node-chain n) s* extra els ths)))))))
  (define ((update-exit b) n)
    (let* ((self (node-state n))
           (ts (nstate-tstamp self))
           (e (node-state (branch-entry b)))
           (result (node-state (branch-active-result b))))
      (values
        (if (or (fx> (nstate-tstamp e) ts) (fx> (nstate-tstamp result) ts))
          (make-nstate
            (let1 (v (nstate-val result))
                  (if (node-inactive? n) v (force-value v)))
            (nstate-ll self)
            (nstate-dst self)
            (chain-now (node-chain n)))
          self)
        '())))
  (define (deactivate lo hi) (modify! (ch) (deactivate! ch lo hi)))
  (define (in-branch entry polarity m)
    (state (λ (ch)
              (let1 (scope (chain-scope ch))
                    (chain-scope-set! ch (cons (cons entry polarity) scope))
                    (let1 (r (if (inactive-scope? ch)
                               (parameterize
                                 ((lazy-lifting #t))
                                 (run-state m ch))
                               (run-state m ch)))
                          (chain-scope-set! (cdr r) scope)
                          r)))))
  (define (ifnode entry th el)
    (do/monad
      (when/monad
        (eq? (node-kind entry) 'resampleable)
        (hakaru10-fail
          'internal
          'if_
          "Entry node for if should not be sampleable"
          entry))
      (let testv (check-test 'if_ (node-value entry) entry))
      (let (,active ,inactive) (if testv (list th el) (list el th)))
      (← added-old (gets chain-count))
      (← inactive-nr (in-branch entry (not testv) inactive))
      (← added-inactive (gets chain-count))
      (deactivate added-old added-inactive)
      (← active-nr (in-branch entry testv active))
      (← added-active (gets chain-count))
      (let b (if
              testv
              (make-branch
                entry
                active-nr
                inactive-nr
                (cons added-inactive added-active)
                (cons added-old added-inactive))
              (make-branch
                entry
                inactive-nr
                active-nr
                (cons added-old added-inactive)
                (cons added-inactive added-active))))
      (← exit
         (create-node
           (update-exit b)
           (dirac (node-value active-nr))
           'exit
           (list->vector
             (delete-duplicates (list entry active-nr inactive-nr) eq?))
           #f))
      (modify! (ch) (node-branch-set! exit b))
      (new-dependency entry exit)
      (new-dependency active-nr exit)
      (new-dependency inactive-nr exit)
      (modify!
        (ch)
        (let1 (prev (node-update entry))
              (when prev (node-update-set! entry (update-entry prev b))))
        (node-branch-set! entry b))
      (return exit)))
  (define (modify-node! n s*)
    (let ((ch (node-chain n)) (old (node-state n)))
      (set-node-state! n s*)
      (unless
        (and (extended? ch)
             (eq? (node-kind n) 'observed)
             (or (node-inactive? n) (node-pending n)))
        (chain-llcum-set!
          ch
          (fp- (fp+ (chain-llcum ch) (nstate-ll s*)) (nstate-ll old))))
      (if (fx= (nstate-tstamp old) (nstate-tstamp s*)) '() (node-deps n))))
  (define (guarded-update upd n)
    (condition-case
      (upd n)
      (e (exn) (if (deferrable? e) (values #f '()) (signal e)))))
  (define (abort-propagation! ch)
    (chain-llcum-set! ch -inf.0)
    (chain-pending-set! ch '())
    (update-queue-clear! (chain-queue ch)))
  (define (propagate! ch)
    (let ((q (chain-queue ch)) (extended (extended? ch)) (now (chain-now ch)))
      (let loop ()
        (if (update-queue-empty? q)
          (settle-pending! ch)
          (let1 (n (dequeue! q))
                (cond ((and (not extended) (node-inactive? n))
                       (set-delayed! n #t)
                       (loop))
                      ((fx>= (nstate-tstamp (node-state n)) now)
                       (hakaru10-error
                         'dependency
                         'propagate
                         "dependency violation: updating the already updated"
                         n))
                      ((node-update n)
                       =>
                       (λ (upd)
                          (receive
                            (s* extra)
                            (if (and extended (node-inactive? n))
                              (guarded-update upd n)
                              (upd n))
                            (if s*
                              (begin
                                (enqueue-all! ch extra)
                                (enqueue-all! ch (modify-node! n s*))
                                (loop))
                              (abort-propagation! ch)))))
                      (else (loop))))))))
  (define hakaru10-debug (make-parameter #f))
  (define-syntax-rule
    (debug-trace arg ...)
    (when (hakaru10-debug)
          (with-output-to-port (current-error-port) (τ (print arg ...)))))
  (define (apply-proposal! n v ll fb)
    (let ((ch (node-chain n)) (s (node-state n)))
      (enqueue-all!
        ch
        (modify-node! n (make-nstate v ll (nstate-dst s) (chain-now ch))))
      fb))
  (define (resample-trace! ch)
    (let* ((samp (chain-samp ch))
           (hi (fx- (vector-length samp) 1))
           (src (chain-rng ch)))
      (let retry ()
        (let1 (n (vector-ref samp (rng-integer! src 0 hi)))
              (if (node-inactive? n)
                (retry)
                (let1 (s (node-state n))
                      (receive
                        (v ll fb)
                        ((rdistr-resample (nstate-dst s))
                         src
                         (nstate-val s)
                         (nstate-ll s))
                        (debug-trace
                          "resample "
                          (node-addr n)
                          ": "
                          (nstate-val s)
                          " -> "
                          v
                          " fb "
                          fb)
                        (apply-proposal! n v ll fb))))))))
  (define (log-acceptance-ratio ch sampleables card-old fb)
    (let1 (card-new (chain-card-change ch))
          (fp+ (fp- (chain-llcum ch) fb)
               (if (fx= card-new card-old)
                 0.0
                 (fplog (fp/ (exact->inexact (fx+ sampleables card-old))
                             (exact->inexact (fx+ sampleables card-new))))))))
  (define (rejected-ll? x) (and (not (nan? x)) (infinite? x)))
  (define (run-step-hook ch accepted? delta)
    (let1 (hook (chain-step-hook ch)) (when hook (hook ch accepted? delta))))
  (define (transit! ch)
    (journal-begin! ch)
    (chain-now-set! ch (fx+ (chain-now ch) 1))
    (chain-llcum-set! ch 0.0)
    (let ((card-old (chain-card-change ch))
          (sampleables (vector-length (chain-samp ch))))
      (if (fx= 0 (fx+ sampleables card-old))
        (begin (journal-commit! ch) (run-step-hook ch #t 0.0) #t)
        (let* ((fb (resample-trace! ch))
               (delta (begin (propagate! ch) (chain-llcum ch)))
               (ratio (and (not (rejected-ll? delta))
                           (log-acceptance-ratio ch sampleables card-old fb)))
               (accept?
                 (and ratio
                      (or (fp>= ratio 0.0)
                          (fp< (rng-double! (chain-rng ch) 0.0 1.0)
                               (fpexp ratio))))))
          (debug-trace
            "step "
            (chain-now ch)
            ": delta "
            delta
            " ratio "
            ratio
            (if accept? " accepted" " rejected"))
          (if accept? (journal-commit! ch) (journal-rollback! ch))
          (run-step-hook ch accept? delta)
          accept?))))
  (define (chain-extended-ll ch)
    (chain-fold
      (λ (n acc)
         (case (node-kind n)
           ((resampleable) (fp+ acc (nstate-ll (node-state n))))
           ((observed)
            (if (node-inactive? n) acc (fp+ acc (nstate-ll (node-state n)))))
           (else acc)))
      0.0
      ch))
  (define (build-chain prog src semantics)
    (check-model prog 'mcmC)
    (let* ((ch (new-chain src semantics))
           (result
             (condition-case
               (parameterize ((lazy-lifting #f)) (eval-state prog ch))
               (e (hakaru10-rejected-build) #f))))
      (chain-freeze! ch)
      (cond ((not result) (chain-llcum-set! ch -inf.0))
            ((extended? ch) (chain-llcum-set! ch (chain-extended-ll ch))))
      (values ch result)))
  (define (initial-step limit prog src semantics)
    (let retry ((limit limit))
      (if (<= limit 0)
        (values #f #f 0)
        (receive
          (ch result)
          (build-chain prog src semantics)
          (if (rejected-ll? (chain-llcum ch))
            (retry (- limit 1))
            (values ch result limit))))))
  (define (mcmc-generator
           limit
           prog
           #!key
           (seed 17)
           (rng #f)
           (semantics 'upstream)
           (step-hook #f)
           (build-hook #f))
    (unless
      (and (exact-integer? limit) (>= limit 0))
      (hakaru10-error
        'construction
        'mcmC
        "limit: must be a non-negative integer"
        limit))
    (receive
      (ch result limit*)
      (initial-step limit prog (or rng (make-stdgen-source seed)) semantics)
      (and ch
           (let1 (started #f)
                 (chain-step-hook-set! ch step-hook)
                 (when build-hook (build-hook ch))
                 (τ (if started (transit! ch) (set! started #t))
                    (force-value (node-value result)))))))
  (define (mcmc-fold kons knil limit prog . keys)
    (let1 (next (apply mcmc-generator limit prog keys))
          (if next
            (let loop ((i 0) (acc knil))
              (if (>= i limit) acc (loop (+ i 1) (kons (next) acc))))
            knil))))

Embedding notes

  • Models are State computations (plan §15 C32, C33). A model is a value of the State monad of (aux category state), Model a = MCMCM (SExp a) (S:82), whose state is the chain: dist, diracN and if_ are models, lift, pair, collect and condition are pure, and return makes no node (S:197, S:211-214). Running a model again makes fresh nodes, so there is no accidental sharing (P l.175-179) and sharing comes only from binding a name (S:53-73). Models compose by binding one another ((phierN′ 9 x 1.) in condhier) and are values (phierN folds a model transformer). The engine's construction functions keep upstream's names, each a State action (create-node, M:404-419; with-node-ctor, S:218-228; ifnode, M:641-686).
  • Bind order is creation order (plan §15 C35). Node creation order is the topological order of propagation and fixes the random draws of a replay. A node is created when the model that makes it runs, in bind order: do/monad top to bottom, and fold/monad, traverse/monad (mapM), for-each/monad (mapM_) and replicate/monad head first. Building a model creates no node, so a model may be built anywhere, in an argument position or inside map: the order is the binds', as ordered-pair shows (case test/language/applicative).
  • if_ delays its branches (plan §15 C36). if_ is syntax over the engine's if_* (S:275-279): its branch expressions are evaluated when ifnode builds each branch, inside the branch's scope and, for the inactive one, under lazy lifting, the stand-in for Haskell's non-strict arguments on which deviation 11 relies. A branch must be a model, (return v) for a value: (if_ c 1 2) raises construction when the branch is built. Being syntax, if_ is used inside a λ where a procedure is needed, as in the foldrs of the colored balls.
  • Running a model several times (plan §15 C35). A model may run several times: the retries of the initial trace (M:882-891), the rebuild of #:check, the trace graph of the oracles. A Scheme effect that must happen once per run belongs inside a bind, after it: the first clause of a top-level do/monad is evaluated once, when the definition is. That is harmless for dist, diracN, if_ and return, which only build models; an SExp computed there, such as (lift car '()), is computed at once (deviation 11). The counters of case B4′ run their effect per run with (>>= (return #f) (λ (_) (add1! k) (return k))), the models of case L5 after the bind of the node they capture, with (let _ (set! captured x)).
  • A clause that is not a model. A bare clause of do/monad must be a model: one that is not, such as (add1! k) between two binds, fails with CHICKEN's record-type error when the model runs, a Scheme error, not a construction one (case L5). Bind it with (let _ ...) instead.
  • The static discipline is the build (plan §15 C37). SExp is applicative, not a monad (S:118), and if_ builds both branches (M:650-661), so one build is the whole trace graph, and it checks the static discipline (deviations 9, 10) as it goes, but for what only a later value can show, which the updates and the oracles' recomputation check: a node's kind, the boolean test of if_, a lifted function's answer, and an observation that a distribution constructor computes from its argument (case L5). exact-posterior, model->search-tree, weighted-samples and likelihood-weighting walk the graph of one 'extended build (plan §6.10), reusing none of the incremental machinery. That assumes a model whose Scheme code does not branch on build-time values, which nothing catches: the rebuild of #:check draws the same values from a copy of the same source, so it takes the same branch, and catches only a construction that depends on state outliving a run (the counters and switching of case B4′).
  • condition. (chicken condition) exports a procedure of the same name: import it as (except (chicken condition) condition), as this file does.
  • Stochastic memoization (P §3 l.531-544, footnote 5 l.566-569, CB:41-44) becomes pre-allocation, as upstream's own balls_prior (CB:43, sequence . replicate): (replicate/monad n m) runs m n times, in order, before the if_s that use its nodes, which so belong to no branch; balls-prior keys them by ball in an alist.
  • Sampling. mcmC, mcmC-fold (constant memory) and mcmC§ (an (aux stream) stream: take§, §->list, car§, cdr§) take the keys #:seed (17, as M:858), #:rng (an rng source, used as is), #:semantics ('upstream or 'extended, see the semantics suite) and #:check (the reference evaluator on every step, or on the first n). There is no burn-in nor thinning: drop the samples, as upstream does.

Findings and deviations

The deviations, each tagged in the source with its number:

  1. the exit's update returns the node unchanged when neither the entry nor the active result is newer, where upstream fails (M:788-792; case S20 if/shared-inactive);
  2. the gamma density uses the scale form; upstream's formula matches no parameterization (D:298-304);
  3. the gamma sampler with a shape below 1 applies the scale once, upstream twice (D:279-282);
  4. a Poisson sample's log-likelihood is its log-density, upstream returns the probability (D:379);
  5. a categorical walk exhausted by rounding returns the last entry; upstream has no case for the empty list (D:99-102);
  6. with no active resampleable node a step is a no-op; upstream loops or crashes (M:962-966; case L7);
  7. a model with no random node is a constant chain, a consequence of 6 (case L6);
  8. the opt-in 'extended semantics, which removes the reactivation bias of upstream's kernel (see the semantics suite);
  9. the static checks of Haskell's types become run-time errors (P l.153-155, l.419-421), among them the boolean test of if_ (S:275); a Model is a value of its own type (plan §15 C32), so a model where an SExp is expected, a model answered by a lifted function, and a model inside a Scheme value (a Val, a Dirac value, or the first answer of a lifted function) are run-time construction errors; a bare non-model clause is a Scheme error; procedures are plain values (S:107); a Val argument of dist holding an SExp is rejected, and so is a constructor applied to the wrong number of arguments (S:137-151; case L5); a sample count must be an exact integer (S:196); and an observation must be external to the model, so one that a distribution constructor computes from its argument raises conditioning at the update that changes it, where upstream's types accept it and its update keeps the first value (S:251-252, M:487-488; case L5);
  10. extra checks with no upstream counterpart: branch escape, as a parent or as the result of a branch or of the model, owner chain, kind stability (M:46-60), the parameter checks of beta, laplace and dirichlet (D:318-328, D:384-391, D:407-411), and a negative sample count, where upstream retries the build forever or answers an infinite list (M:885-891, M:897-898; case L5);
  11. strict propagation of a deferred value: the value of an inactive Dirac node whose computation raises is deferred, as upstream's lazy values are (M:99, M:120-129), but a function applied to it in an inactive branch is not called, so a strict reader of the result raises the deferred error where upstream computes a value that ignores it (S:100-116; case B6). Scheme is strict, so if_ delays its branch models until ifnode builds them (plan §15 C36), where a partial lift of an inactive branch is deferred; an SExp computed outside any branch, e.g. by a top-level (define m (diracN (lift car '()))), raises at once where upstream raises when the value is forced. Likewise an active diracN or exit, or a lift of Vals in an active branch, computes its value at once, where upstream forces it only when it is read: when it is made or updated (case B6, strict-active and strict-active/val), and when a step activates it holding a deferred value that no update of the step recomputes (case H5 of hakaru10-exact.scm).

Kept as upstream, with a comment in the source: the reactivation bias in 'upstream mode (M:1159-1165), the categorical duplicate-key asymmetry (the sampler sums, the density takes the first match, D:98), beta raising an error outside [0, 1] (D:331-337), NaN on deactivating an observed node of LL -inf, a NaN ratio drawing u and rejecting, and the empty chain when the initial retries run out (M:868).

Upstream's own records that this port does not reproduce, because they come from older revisions of the engine: tac1-3's 620/523/532 (S:346-368; the three chains are identical here: tac2 and tac3 as S:370-371 says they should be, and tac1 too, since a constant Dirac node draws nothing, X16), phier_kl's 0.7863/0.2362/0.03442/0.002157 (E:76-85; the transcription gives 0.78688 and 0.23811 for the first two), Bench's 1e4 line (BE:28-29, -3.151396208304257 and 54.17557680794696; -3.151835923727823 and 54.168535367816084 here and in the transcription), and the older mixng KLs 2.276e-2 and 1.356e-2 (E:153-162). Likewise, presumably from older revisions of the engine: the sums of 1000 samples of nice_prog_mult_conditions, 499.799988743311 and 257.2928205920028 (S:297-301), are 502.706370138924 and 252.485601387305 here, although the chain of (biased-coin #t #f), identical to the first one's, replays E:208-210 bit for bit (X11); niceex1's 7 samples are "All True" (S:288-289), here (#t #t #t #t #f #f #f); pcond3's first record (1,5011), (20,2507), (21,2482) (E:135), next to the "new" one that X6 replays; the "incremental" moments of test_multiple_conditions13, (0.2445212550806874, 3.651432172401049e-2) (E:219-220, commented out upstream, of a prog_multiple_conditions defined nowhere), here (0.251523121569678, 0.0379368207086475) for (biased-coin #f #f); and exgrass/exgrass' of the low-level node API, not ported, whose record is [0.9, 0.9, 0.9, 0.9, 0.8, 0.1, 0.1, 0.1, 0.1, 0.8] (M:808-809, M:821-822), where their transcription into models (rain' and sprinkler' as diracN) gives (0.9 0.9 0.9 0.8 0.8 0.1 0.1 0.8 0.8 0.1) with the default kernel. The "original Metropolis" moments of E:207-208 and E:217-218 are those of the old code that M:1174-1415 keeps commented out "for reference", which is not ported. The records E:94 (5809), E:102 (2026), P l.217-218 (4 True, for E:10-12) and E:157 (1.1301558326703463e-2) were made with the always-flip kernel of D:64-70, commented out upstream and kept here as bern/flip.

Paper errata: P l.695 gives the LL of x = 0.1 under N(0, 1) as -0.616, it is -0.9239385332046727; P l.363 writes dist gamma (1/3) for gamma 3 (1/3) (E:144); P l.655-658 lacks a dist; P l.261 says diracN "has the alias return", but return creates no node (S:197, S:211-214): the two agree only through the unit laws.

Related work

From P §5 (l.870-902): Figaro [18], an embedded DSL in Scala that also builds a trace graph updated by MH, apparently without incremental evaluation; Infer.NET [15], which builds a graph but relies on expectation propagation; Ścibior et al. [20], Haskell and MCMC but a monad, so the construction is too dynamic for static conditioning checks (and it can express cyclic models); Anglican [22] and more advanced MCMC algorithms; Yang et al. [23], incremental MH by generating code from the initial trace; Hur et al. [10], a provably correct MH for an imperative language, whose examples (Fig. 2 and 3) are mixng and phier here; their Fig. 1, start_example (E:324-336, commented out upstream), conditions on the boolean formula observe (x || y), which Hakaru10 forbids by design (P l.393-398, S:43-49), and is not ported. Hakaru10 is a rewrite of the original Hakaru [24], fixing the problems of the lightweight implementation of Wingate et al. [21] pointed out in [11] (P §1 l.137-143, 175-183): the unit law of Dirac, accidental sharing of random primitives, and conditioning inside branches.

The exact oracle of these tests reuses Hansei, the embedded probabilistic DSL of Kiselyov and Shan (aux.hansei.scm): its lazy search trees and their exploration. The public module:

          dist

          build-chain

          node-kind
          node-role
          node-state
          node-inactive
          node-inactive?
          node-delayed
          node-pending)
    (only (aux hakaru10 exact)
          exact-posterior
          model->search-tree
          likelihood-weighting
          weighted-samples
          construction-check
          reference-check))
  (define-syntax-rule
    (if_ test then else)
    (if_* test (lazy-model 'if_ (τ then)) (lazy-model 'if_ (τ else))))
  (define condition condition/external)
  (define (lift f . es) (lift* f es))
  (define sexp-map lift)
  (define (sexp+ . es) (lift* + es))
  (define (sexp- . es) (lift* - es))
  (define (sexp* . es) (lift* * es))
  (define (sexp/ . es) (lift* / es))
  (define-record step-snapshot now card-change ll nodes)
  (define (take-step-snapshot ch)
    (make-step-snapshot
      (chain-now ch)
      (chain-card-change ch)
      (chain-extended-ll ch)
      (chain-fold
        (λ (n acc)
           (cons (list (node-state n)
                       (node-inactive n)
                       (node-delayed n)
                       (node-pending n))
                 acc))
        '()
        ch)))
  (define (same-state? a b)
    (and (= (step-snapshot-now a) (step-snapshot-now b))
         (= (step-snapshot-card-change a) (step-snapshot-card-change b))
         (every (λ (m n) (every eq? m n))
                (step-snapshot-nodes a)
                (step-snapshot-nodes b))))
  (define (check-step before ch accepted? delta)
    (let1 (after (take-step-snapshot ch))
          (cond ((not accepted?)
                 (unless
                   (same-state? before after)
                   (hakaru10-error
                     'internal
                     'check
                     "a rejected step did not restore the state"
                     (step-snapshot-now before))))
                ((and (extended? ch)
                      (finite? (step-snapshot-ll before))
                      (finite? (step-snapshot-ll after)))
                 (let* ((ll0 (step-snapshot-ll before))
                        (expected (- (step-snapshot-ll after) ll0)))
                   (unless
                     (<= (abs (- delta expected))
                         (* 1e-09 (max 1.0 (abs ll0))))
                     (hakaru10-error
                       'internal
                       'check
                       "the step's delta is not the extended LL change"
                       (chain-now ch)
                       delta
                       expected)))))))
  (define (check-hooks check)
    (let ((before #f) (steps 0))
      (define (more?) (or (eq? check #t) (< steps check)))
      (define (verify! ch)
        (reference-check ch)
        (if (more?)
          (set! before (take-step-snapshot ch))
          (chain-step-hook-set! ch #f)))
      (values
        verify!
        (λ (ch accepted? delta)
           (check-step before ch accepted? delta)
           (add1! steps)
           (verify! ch)))))
  (define (with-chain-keys driver model seed rng semantics check)
    (unless
      (or (boolean? check) (and (exact-integer? check) (>= check 0)))
      (hakaru10-error
        'construction
        'mcmC
        "check: must be a boolean or a step count"
        check))
    (let1 (src (or rng (make-stdgen-source seed)))
          (if check
            (begin
              (construction-check
                model
                #:rng
                (rng-source-copy src)
                #:semantics
                semantics)
              (receive
                (build-hook step-hook)
                (check-hooks check)
                (driver
                  #:rng
                  src
                  #:semantics
                  semantics
                  #:build-hook
                  build-hook
                  #:step-hook
                  step-hook)))
            (driver #:rng src #:semantics semantics))))
  (define (mcmC-fold
           kons
           knil
           limit
           model
           #!key
           (seed 17)
           (rng #f)
           (semantics 'upstream)
           (check #f))
    (let1 (prog (model->trace model 'mcmC))
          (with-chain-keys
            (λ keys (apply mcmc-fold kons knil limit prog keys))
            model
            seed
            rng
            semantics
            check)))
  (define (mcmC limit model . keys)
    (reverse (apply mcmC-fold cons '() limit model keys)))
  (define (mcmC§
           model
           #!key
           (seed 17)
           (rng #f)
           (semantics 'upstream)
           (check #f)
           (retries 1000))
    (let* ((prog (model->trace model 'mcmC))
           (next (with-chain-keys
                   (λ keys (apply mcmc-generator retries prog keys))
                   model
                   seed
                   rng
                   semantics
                   check)))
      (if next (τ->§ next) '())))
  (define (model-graph model #!key (seed 17) (rng #f) (semantics 'upstream))
    (receive
      (ch result)
      (build-chain
        (model->trace model 'model-graph)
        (or rng (make-stdgen-source seed))
        semantics)
      (define (count-nodes node?)
        (chain-fold (λ (n k) (if (node? n) (add1 k) k)) 0 ch))
      (define ((kind? kind) n) (eq? (node-kind n) kind))
      `((nodes unquote (chain-count ch))
        (resampleable unquote (count-nodes (kind? 'resampleable)))
        (observed unquote (count-nodes (kind? 'observed)))
        (dirac unquote (count-nodes (kind? 'dirac)))
        (inactive unquote (count-nodes node-inactive?))
        (entries unquote (count-nodes (λ (n) (eq? (node-role n) 'entry))))))))

test/language/graph/regression: pass

L2: the Bayesian linear regression of P §3 (l.461-504) with five parameters and 500 points: "its graph has 511 vertices (there are five hundred observations and five parameters)" (P l.493-495), here 512 with the Dirac node of the result, collect w. The noise's (1/) <$> is a lifted expression, not a node.

(define (normals means std) (traverse/monad (λ (m) (dist normal m std)) means))

(define (dot xs ays)
  (reduce-right sexp+ #f (map (λ (x ay) (lift (λ (a) (* a x)) ay)) xs ays)))

(define (regression xsy)
  (do/monad
    (let mu (make-list (length (caar xsy)) 0.0))
    (← w-mean (normals mu 2.0))
    (← w (normals w-mean 1.0))
    (← noise-sd (<$> (cut lift (λ (g) (/ 1.0 g)) <>) (dist gamma 0.5 0.5)))
    (traverse/monad
      (Λ ((,xs unquote y)) (dist (condition y normal) (dot xs w) noise-sd))
      xsy)
    (return (collect w))))

-- normals means std
-- returns several independently distributed normal random variables
-- whose means are given by the list means, with the same std
normals :: [SExp Double] -> SExp Double -> MCMCM [SExp Double]
normals means std = mapM (\m -> dist normal m std) means


dot :: [Double] -> [SExp Double] -> SExp Double
dot xs ays = foldr1 (+) $ zipWith (\x ay -> (*x) <$> ay) xs ays

collect :: [SExp a] -> SExp [a]
collect = sequenceA

(Birds1.hs:103-114.) The model, as printed in P l.471-486 (re-indented, and with the underscores the PDF's text extraction drops restored):

type RegrDatum = ([Double],Double) -- Xs and Y
model :: [RegrDatum] -> Model [Double]
model xsy = do
  let mu = replicate dimK 0
  w_mean   <- normals mu 2
  w        <- normals w_mean 1
  noise_sd <- (1/) <$> dist gamma 0.5 0.5
  let make_cond (xs,y) = dist (y `condition` normal) (dot xs w) noise_sd
  mapM make_cond xsy
  return $ collect w

test/language/graph/colored-balls: pass

L3: the colored balls of BLOG (CB:1-13, P §3 l.505-616) with 8 possible balls and 10 blue draws: 2114 nodes, 89 resampleable (8 colors, the number of balls and one drawn ball per draw and per number of balls, 8 + 1 + 10 × 8), and 1994 of them inactive in the initial trace at seed 17 (prototype regression value).

(define (opposite-color color) (if (eq? color 'blue) 'green 'blue))

(define (observed-color color)
  (categorical `((,color . 0.8) (,(opposite-color color) . 0.2))))

(define max-balls 8)

(define (balls-prior n)
  (do/monad
    (← balls (replicate/monad n (dist uniformly '(blue green))))
    (return (map cons (iota n 1) balls))))

(define (balls-observe balls obs nballs)
  (>> (for-each/monad
        (λ (color)
           (do/monad
             (← b (dist uniformly (iota nballs 1)))
             (fold-right
               (λ (i rest)
                  (if_ (lift (λ (bv) (= bv i)) b)
                       (dist (condition color observed-color)
                             (alist-ref i balls))
                       rest))
               (return color)
               (iota max-balls 1))))
        obs)
      (return '())))

(define (cballs-model obs)
  (do/monad
    (← balls (balls-prior max-balls))
    (← nballs (dist uniformly (iota max-balls 1)))
    (fold-right
      (λ (i rest)
         (if_ (lift (λ (n) (= n i)) nballs) (balls-observe balls obs i) rest))
      (return '())
      (iota max-balls 1))
    (return nballs)))

-- Submodel: Given balls color and the observations, perform conditioning
balls_observe :: M.IntMap (SExp Color) -> [Color] -> Int -> Model ()
balls_observe balls obs nballs = mapM_ observe_ball obs >> return (pure ())
 where
  -- The same ball can be drawn twice as we return the drawn balls
  -- into the urn. A ball keeps its true color, no matter how many
  -- times it is drawn.
   observe_ball color = do
        b <- dist uniformly (pure [1..nballs])
        -- It is more efficient to create if statements, rather than
        -- create an SExp(IntMap Color).
        let observe i =
              let true_color = balls ! i in
              if_ ((== i) <$> b) $
                dist (color `condition` observed_color) true_color
        foldr observe (return (pure color)) [1..maxBalls]

-- The model takes a list of observations as an argument
cballs_model :: [Color] -> Model Int
cballs_model obs = do
  balls  <- balls_prior maxBalls
  nballs <- dist uniformly (pure [1..maxBalls])
  let obs_number i =
        if_ ((== i) <$> nballs) $ balls_observe balls obs i
  foldr obs_number (return (pure())) [1..maxBalls]
  return nballs

(ColoredBalls.hs:74-99.) if_ test $ m is if_ short of its else branch, which the foldr supplies: a chain of nested if_s, built by fold-right as upstream's foldr (if_ being syntax, inside a λ).

        (⊦ equal?

test/language/applicative: pass

The rest of the applicative surface (S:99-135). The Model applicative, pair <$> dist normal 0 1 <*> dist normal 5 1, runs its arguments left to right, so ordered-pair creates its nodes in the order of ordered-pair* and replays it sample for sample, while swapped-pair, the same distribution with the nodes made in the other order, draws other samples: node creation order is observable (plan C18, §15 C35).

(define ordered-pair
  (<*> (<$> (λ (a) (cut pair a <>)) (dist normal 0.0 1.0))
       (dist normal 5.0 1.0)))

(define ordered-pair*
  (do/monad
    (← a (dist normal 0.0 1.0))
    (← b (dist normal 5.0 1.0))
    (return (pair a b))))

(define swapped-pair
  (do/monad
    (← b (dist normal 5.0 1.0))
    (← a (dist normal 0.0 1.0))
    (return (pair a b))))

sexp- and sexp/ of one argument are negate and recip (S:125, S:131), of two - and / (S:123, S:130); sexp-map is fmap (S:99-102), evaluated now over plain values, a procedure among them (S:107 Val f); and the engine's sexp? holds of nodes and lifted expressions only (S:85-89).

(define (test/language/applicative _)
  (⊦ equal? (mcmC 50 ordered-pair*) (mcmC 50 ordered-pair))
  (⊦≠ (mcmC 50 swapped-pair) (mcmC 50 ordered-pair))
  (⊦ equal?
     '(-3.0 0.25 2 0.5 4 #f)
     (list (sexp- 3.0)
           (sexp/ 4.0)
           (sexp- 3 1)
           (sexp/ 1.0 2.0)
           (sexp-map add1 3)
           (sexp? 3)))
  (⊦ equal?
     '((-3.0) (0.25))
     (list (mcmC 1 (return (sexp- 3.0))) (mcmC 1 (return (sexp/ 4.0)))))
  (⊦ = 4 (sexp-map (λ (f v) (f v)) add1 3))
  (for-each
    (Λ ((,x ,x? ,nx? ,neg ,inv ,diff ,quot ,mapped ,applied))
       (⊦ equal?
          (list #t #t (- x) (/ x) (- 10.0 x) (/ x 2.0) (- x) (- x))
          (list x? nx? neg inv diff quot mapped applied)))
    (mcmC 20
          (do/monad
            (← x (dist normal 0.0 1.0))
            (return
              (lift list
                    x
                    (sexp? x)
                    (sexp? (sexp- x))
                    (sexp- x)
                    (sexp/ x)
                    (sexp- 10.0 x)
                    (sexp/ x 2.0)
                    (sexp-map - x)
                    (sexp-map (λ (f v) (f v)) - x)))))))

-- NB: if x is just a NoderRef, (diracN x) returns this reference (shares)

diracN :: Show a => SExp a -> Model a
diracN (Val x)  = fmap NRef $ create_node Nothing (diracS x)

diracN x = fmap NRef $ with_node_ctor x diracS

biased_coinX c1 c2 = do

{-
